
Multiplatform openssl initiation via lib call for multi-key
-----------------------------------------------------------
Calibration to work out numbers for approx 10 mins per each of 17 types, for the main/full run.
Smoke test to validate each type is working. Mybe 20-30 mins.
Full Run is 17x keygens ~10 mins each (min 5 keys), x3 independent runs. About a day per platform.

This excludes the startup time to call openssl externally, vs E2(a) which initiates each time.
Self-calibrates for 2s for smoke and 10s for main runs so no need for additional calibration.


== Summary of results -- in-process library call (E2b) ==
Energy per key generated (J per 1000 keys), paired LIVE minus NULL; key generation called directly in the OpenSSL library from one running process.


== Template ==
-- Smoke --
   Smoke Commands and Screen Outputs
   Smoke File and Outputs
-- Full Run --
   Full Commands and Screen Outputs
   Full File and Outputs
